Abstract: Relativistic jets embedded in active galactic nucleus (AGN) accretion disks are promising high-energy neutrino sources, but their emission is often estimated from a single representative jet state. We develop a time-dependent framework that follows jet-head propagation, evolving reverse-shock conditions, and particle cooling until the jet chokes or breaks out, and apply it to the SG and TQM disk models. For the representative choked cases, neutrino emission is dominated by the high-dissipation phase near jet stalling, allowing a stalling-state approximation to reproduce the trajectory-integrated, detector-weighted event yield within approximately $14\%$. In breakout cases, however, rapid jet-head acceleration across steep disk-density gradients suppresses reverse-shock dissipation and can cause single-state estimates to overpredict the fluence, even after accounting for the available energy budget. Full trajectory integration also reshapes the high-energy spectral tail and produces distinct detectability patterns across SMBH mass and disk radius for the two disk models. Some lower-density outer-disk cases develop harder tails extending into the 10--100 PeV range, motivating future ultra-high-energy neutrino searches. Resolving jet propagation dynamics is therefore indispensable for evaluating embedded transients across AGN disk environments and avoiding systematic biases in multi-messenger modeling.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重球GMRES（HBGMRES）方法结合了重启GMRES和优化中的重球方法，既能限制内存使用和控制正交化成本，又能掩盖重启GMRES收敛慢的问题。然而另一种Krylov子空间方法GMRESR（以GCR为外层算法、GMRES为内层方法）虽然能对由GMRES获得的新搜索向量和GCR中所有先前保留的搜索向量给出近似最优解，但其收敛速度与GMRES一样慢。作者希望设计一种方法，能够同时保留GMRESR的全局最优性优势并解决其收敛慢的缺陷。

Method: 作者受HBGMRES启发，提出了重球GMRESR方法（HBGMRESR）。核心思路是将GMRESR内部使用的GMRES替换为HBGMRES作为内层迭代，从而在保留GMRESR在特定Krylov子空间部分进行全局最小化优势的同时，利用HBGMRES的重球特性恢复失去的收敛速度。新方法通过两层迭代结构的协同作用实现收敛性与最优性的平衡。

Result: 在真实数据上的数值测试表明，新提出的HBGMRESR方法在收敛性能上优于GMRESR和HBGMRES方法，验证了该方法在收敛速度方面的显著优势。

Abstract: The heavy ball GMRES (HBGMRES) method is one of Krylov subspace methods for linear systems combined with the restarted GMRES and the heavy ball method which is applied in optimization. HBGMRES not only keeps benefit of the restarted GMRES in limiting memory usage and controlling orthogonalization cost, but also is able to cover up the slow convergence problem in the restarted GMRES. Another type of Krylov subspace methods is GMRESR which consists of GCR as the outer algorithm and certain steps of GMRES as an inner method. Compared with HBGMRES, GMRESR gives the approximately optimal solution over the new search vector gained from GMRES and all previously kept search vectors in GCR. Even though GMRESR performs better than HBGMRES, it has slow convergence which is similar to GMRES and the restarted GMRES. Inspired by HBGMRES, we present the heavy ball GMRESR method (HBGMRESR) by using HBGMRES as the inner loop instead of using GMRES to salvage the lost convergence speed while still keeping the benefit of GMRESR that in the sense of a global minimization over some specific part of the Krylov subspace is done. Numerical tests on real data are presented to demonstratee the superiority of the new methods over GMRESR and HBGMRES.

Abstract: Maximum-angle conditions are standard finite-element mesh hypotheses that permit anisotropic triangles excluded by minimum-angle or shape-regularity assumptions. We identify the support-plane geometry linking the chordal circumradius to radial distortion and derive sharp comparisons between the intrinsic spherical and chordal semi-regularity parameters. In particular, a locality-independent comparison holds with sharp constant $2/\sqrt3$ and a characterised equality case. An area-based parameter is shown to be smaller than the spherical semi-regularity parameter, with sharp constant one in the local flat limit. For spherical finite-element meshes, the vertex criterion implies uniform chordal semi-regularity with an explicit constant, while relative refinement makes the radial-distortion factors converge uniformly to one. Thus, the intrinsic criterion, together with relative refinement, provides the geometric controls used in anisotropic finite-element analysis without imposing a minimum-angle or shape-regularity condition.

Abstract: We introduce a simple and performant approach for rapidly and accurately performing polyharmonic spline (PHS) interpolation using a combination of the fast multipole method (FMM) from computational electrostatics and the Vecchia approximation from the Gaussian process and sparse approximate inverse literatures. Abstract: We study the implementable finite-batch particle algorithm for mean-field variational inference as a fully discrete stochastic approximation of the projected Wasserstein dynamics. The target potential is globally smooth but need not be strongly convex. The departure from contractivity is quantified by the curvature defect \[ \mathfrak d_α(x,y) =
\bigl[α\|x-y\|^2- \langle\nabla V(x)-\nabla V(y),x-y\rangle\bigr]_+, \] which is the additive loss in the one-step Euler contraction estimate. We prove a non-asymptotic Wasserstein stability bound that separates initialization, product-empirical approximation, finite-batch drift error, time discretization, and the defects accumulated along the coupled trajectories. Under the uniform bound $\mathfrak d_α\leqβ$, the particle iterates remain within $O(\sqrt{β/α})$ of any MFVI minimizer, up to explicit errors in the particle number, batch size, and step size. The proof uses a stationary comparison array whose population law is an MFVI minimizer but whose particle-level law is a random product empirical measure, and it controls the resulting projected-drift discrepancy explicitly. We also give coordinatewise defect estimates and structural conditions for dimension-independent projected-drift sensitivity, construct an arbitrary-dimensional smooth nonconvex benchmark with a closed-form MFVI minimizer, and explain why polynomially growing drifts require a modification of the untamed explicit scheme.

Abstract: We develop a moving-contour Hankel framework for encoding local zero configurations of the Riemann $Ξ$-function. Weighted contour integrals of the logarithmic derivative $Ξ'/Ξ$, expressed in a holomorphic coordinate associated with the contour, are identified with the power moments of a finite atomic measure supported at the coordinate images of the enclosed zeros. This representation yields exact zero-free and rank criteria and, for conjugation-compatible contour-coordinate pairs, an inertia formula: once the matrix order is at least the number of distinct coordinate nodes, the negative index equals the number of distinct nonreal conjugate pairs. Consequently, the Riemann hypothesis admits a local finite-dimensional Hankel-positivity formulation, although establishing this positivity independently of the zero set remains unresolved. As the contour moves, the Hankel matrix evolves by a continuous congruence flow between zero crossings and undergoes finite-rank jumps at crossing events. An isolated zero produces a signed rank-one jump, whereas a nonreal conjugate pair produces a rank-two indefinite event in a real-axis circular scan that meets the pair. Removing the continuous coordinate drift yields a piecewise-constant matrix process from which crossing coordinates, multiplicities, and zero locations can be recovered. Numerical experiments validate the contour quadrature, indicator fields, continuous flow, crossing signatures, and recovery procedure.

</details>

Abstract: Pointwise error analysis of discontinuous Galerkin (DG) methods has received considerable attention during the past two decades. Existing analyses establish optimal error estimates in the $L^{\infty}$ norm and the broken $W^{1,\infty}$ seminorm for several DG methods.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数值分析中求积点的几何性质已被广泛研究，但广义求积法（以积分沿曲线代替离散点）的几何性质尚缺乏系统分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，为正广义求积法与多元三角多项式求积提供理论保证，并验证Korobov曲线的准最优性。

Method: 采用符号局部化测试函数（sign-localized test functions）技术推导覆盖半径上界，该方法可自然推广到区间上的代数多项式、球面多项式及双曲交叉三角多项式。对于求积曲线长度的下界，比较了两种近期方法在多元三角多项式背景下的适用性。第二部分通过多个最优性准则分析rank-1 Korobov格规则底层曲线的几何性质，并展示其向二维球面情形的推广。

Result: 获得了正广义求积法覆盖半径的上界，通过符号局部化测试函数技术实现，并建立了求积曲线长度的下界比较结果。证明了rank-1 Korobov格规则所对应的几何简单曲线在多个最优性判据下是准最优的，且该结果可推广至2-球面。

Conclusion: 本文为正广义求积法的几何分析提供了统一的理论框架，揭示了符号局部化测试函数技术在多种多项式空间中的普适性，同时确证了Korobov曲线的理论最优性，为实际数值计算中广义求积法的设计与分析提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The geometric properties of quadrature points in one and multiple dimensions are a classical topic in numerical analysis. Recently, generalized quadrature methods, where discrete points and weights are replaced by integration along curves, have attracted growing interest. This paper focuses on positive generalized quadratures that are exact for multivariate trigonometric polynomials. We derive upper bounds on the covering radius of such quadratures using sign-localized test functions, a technique that also extends naturally to algebraic polynomials on intervals, spherical polynomials, and hyperbolic cross trigonometric polynomials. For lower bounds on the length of quadrature curves, we compare two recent approaches in the context of multivariate trigonometric polynomials. The second part of the paper studies a well-known, geometrically simple curve that underlies rank-1 Korobov lattice rules for periodic functions. We prove that these curves are quasi-optimal with respect to multiple optimality criteria and demonstrate its adaptability to the 2-sphere.

</details>


### [88] [A Positivity-Preserving Expectation Scheme for Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman Equations with Oblique Robin Boundary Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11936)
*Haoran Xu,Xingye Yue*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于条件期望（而非代数模板分解）的保正性数值格式，用于求解带有受控斜Robin边界条件的（可能退化的）Hamilton–Jacobi–Bellman方程。该方法利用反射Feynman–Kac恒等式构造内节点格式，通过Rademacher向量生成2^m个等概率弱Euler分支，并利用斜投影反射处理边界，Robin系数仅通过衰减因子进入。边界节点采用同层闭合格式，在无需对角线占优或CFL条件的假设下，保证了非负数据的正性保持，并得到稀疏非奇异M-矩阵系统或单调压缩算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 各向异性扩散（含混合导数）的标准坐标对齐紧凑模板及局部有限体积/有限元构造，在缺乏适当的系数或网格条件时可能丧失非负系数，导致数值解不保正。现有方法多依赖代数模板分解，难以在一般条件下保持正性。作者希望直接从条件期望出发构造保正格式，避免上述限制。

Method: 内节点：基于一步反射Feynman–Kac恒等式，用m维Rademacher向量生成P=2^m个等概率弱Euler分支；对越界分支，通过斜投影镜像其回退，并将两倍超出量作为离散边界局部时D；Robin系数仅通过衰减因子e^{-κD}和De^{-κD/2}g进入，分支概率保持不变。边界节点：采用独立的空间偏移ℓ_h≍h的同层闭合格式（可为隐式）；利用均匀斜性和P1插值获得与网格无关的内部节点正权重。对固定线性Robin数据，得到稀疏非奇异M-矩阵系统；对一般控制集，得到单调压缩映射。

Result: 格式在不需要对角线占优条件、也不需要Δt与h之间任何CFL型关系的假设下，对非负初值数据保持正性。固定线性Robin数据情形得到M-矩阵系统，一般控制集情形得到单调压缩算子，均保证了数值解的适定性。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了从条件期望（随机分支）视角构造保正方案的有效性，绕过了传统代数模板分解的限制，为各向异性扩散及退化HJB方程的保正数值求解提供了新的途径。算法无需CFL条件和对角线占优假设，具有更广泛的适用性。

Abstract: For anisotropic diffusion with mixed derivatives, standard compact
  coordinate-aligned stencils and some local finite-volume or finite-element
  constructions can lose nonnegative coefficients unless suitable coefficient
  or mesh conditions are imposed. Here nonnegative coefficients are generated
  directly from conditional expectation rather than from an algebraic stencil
  decomposition. We construct a positivity-preserving scheme for
  possibly degenerate Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equations with controlled
  oblique Robin boundary conditions. At interior nodes the construction stems
  from a one-step reflected Feynman--Kac identity: an $m$-dimensional Rademacher
  vector generates $P=2^m$ equally probable weak-Euler branches, an exterior
  branch is mirrored about its oblique projection, and twice its overshoot
  serves as the discrete boundary local time $D$. The Robin coefficient enters
  only through the attenuation factors $e^{-κD}$ and $De^{-κD/2}g$,
  leaving the equal branch probabilities unchanged. Boundary nodes use a
  separate same-level closure over a spatial offset $\ell_h\asymp h$, which may
  be implicit; uniform obliqueness and $\mathbb P_1$ interpolation give a
  mesh-independent positive weight on interior nodes, so fixed linear Robin data
  yield a sparse nonsingular $M$-matrix system and the general control set
  yields a monotone contraction. Positivity is preserved for nonnegative data
  without a diagonal-dominance condition and without any CFL-type relation
  between $Δt$ and $h$.

</details>


### [89] [Numerical methods for the simulation of quantum walks and quantum annealing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12058)
*Asa Hopkins,Viv Kendon*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文分析了切比雪夫多项式近似与真正极小极大近似在Hermitian矩阵函数计算中的差异，并提供了三种近似方法的C++开源实现及严格的误差分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅讨论了切比雪夫多项式近似对Hermitian矩阵函数计算的近最优收敛率，但未涉及真正的极小极大（minimax）近似方案，也未给出高性能实现的细节和严格的误差分析方法。

Method: 对三种近似方法（包括切比雪夫多项式近似和真正的极小极大近似）进行了严格的误差分析，并在C++中实现了高性能、开源的计算代码。

Result: 提供了三种近似方法的完整实现和严格误差分析，涵盖了Hermitian矩阵函数计算中的收敛性和数值精度保证。

Conclusion: 真正的最小极大近似方案不仅在理论上可行，而且可以通过高性能的C++实现获得具有严格误差保证的计算结果，优于仅依赖切比雪夫近似的方案。

Abstract: It is known that Chebyshev based polynomial approximation gives a near-optimal rate of convergence for calculating a function of a Hermitian matrix. However, previous work has not discussed the option of true minimax approximation, nor the specifics of writing a high performance implementation with a rigorous analysis of errors. This work provides such an analysis and an open-source implementation of three approximation methods in C++.

</details>


### [90] [Halving the size of skew-symmetric eigenvalue problems via the polar decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12153)
*Daniel Kressner,Simon Mataigne*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种计算稠密实反对称矩阵特征值问题的新算法，核心思想是利用同时满足反对称和正交性质的极因子，将原问题转化为一半规模的Hermitian特征值问题，从而可用LAPACK等标准软件高效求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实反对称矩阵的特征值问题在数值线性代数中具有重要应用，但现有算法在效率和稳定性方面仍有提升空间。通过将其化为规模减半的Hermitian特征值问题，可以利用成熟的标准软件（如LAPACK）来获得准确且高效的结果。

Method: 计算实反对称矩阵A的极因子（该极因子同时是反对称和正交的），利用该极因子将原始的反对称特征值问题变换为一半规模的Hermitian特征值问题，再通过LAPACK等标准软件求解。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法具有良好的数值稳定性，运行时间与现有反对称特征值求解方法相比具有竞争力；此外，相同的原理还可用于将正交特征值问题约化为一半规模的酉特征值问题。

Conclusion: 该算法为稠密实反对称矩阵特征值问题提供了一种稳定且高效的新途径，通过极因子降维策略将问题转化为标准Hermitian特征值问题，同时该思想具有更广泛的应用潜力（如正交特征值问题）。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel algorithm for computing eigenvalues and eigenvectors of a dense real skew-symmetric matrix $A$. Its main ingredient is the computation of a polar factor of $A$ that is both skew-symmetric and orthogonal. This polar factor is then used to transform the original problem into a Hermitian eigenvalue problem of half the size, which can be solved accurately and efficiently with standard software such as LAPACK. Numerical experiments demonstrate the stability of the method and show that its running time is competitive with existing approaches for skew-symmetric eigenvalue problems. Finally, we show that the same principle can be used to reduce an orthogonal eigenvalue problem to a unitary eigenvalue problem of half the size.

</details>

### [91] [Adaptation of Generalist Robot Policies with Minimal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11363)
*Shreyas Kowshik,Sreyas Venkataraman,Leo Wang,Niharika Pant,Max Simchowitz,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MiDAS提出了一种极简数据适应（minimal-data adaptation）框架，通过先对预训练VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型在单个/少量示范上进行行为克隆锚定，再使用基于残差策略参数化的值函数在线强化学习进行改进，使得机器人能够从单条示范中自主学习和适应新任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习的核心目标是从任务特定的人类数据收集转向通过自主交互实现持续改进，但完全自主学习仍面临稀疏奖励和弱零样本探索的挑战，导致机器人难以从零开始发现成功行为。因此需要一个最接近完全自主改进的可追踪代理框架，研究最少的人类引导能否启动自主学习和所需的关键算法要素。

Method: MiDAS采用简单的离策略到在策略强化学习（offline-to-online RL）方案：1) 通过行为克隆（BC）在单个/少量示范上将预训练的VLA模型锚定到目标任务；2) 在残差策略参数化上使用基于值函数的在线强化学习进一步改进策略。该方法在LIBERO和RoboCasa基准上以及真实双机械臂YAM平台上进行了验证。

Result: 在LIBERO和RoboCasa基准上，MiDAS从单条示范即恢复出强任务性能，大幅优于基线方法，并能泛化到示范条件之外。在真实双机械臂YAM平台上，从单条示范获得的脆弱低成功率策略出发，MiDAS在约6小时的在线交互中提升了策略鲁棒性并学会了新的成功行为。

Conclusion: 该工作首次证明了从单条任务示范实现可靠的机器人策略适应，表明最小人类引导结合精密设计的离线到在线RL配方能够有效引导机器人自主学习和改进。

Abstract: A central goal in robot learning is to move beyond task-specific human data collection toward robots that improve through autonomous interaction. Yet fully autonomous learning remains difficult with current policies: sparse rewards and weak zero-shot exploration make it unlikely that a robot will discover successful behavior from scratch. We study minimal-data adaptation, a regime in which a pre-trained robot policy must learn a new task from as little as one demonstration followed by autonomous online interaction. This setting serves as the closest tractable proxy for fully autonomous improvement, allowing us to study whether minimal human guidance can bootstrap autonomous learning and what algorithmic ingredients make it feasible. We build MiDAS, a simple offline-to-online RL recipe that first anchors a pre-trained VLA to the target task with behavior cloning on single/few demonstrations, then improves it through value-based online RL on a residual policy parameterization. Across LIBERO and RoboCasa, MiDAS recovers strong task performance from as little as one demonstration, substantially outperforming baselines and generalizing beyond demonstrated conditions. We further evaluate MiDAS on a bimanual YAM platform. Starting from a fragile low-success policy obtained from a single demonstration, MiDAS improves its robustness and learns new successful behaviors over ~6 hours of online interaction. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first demonstration of reliable robot policy adaptation from a single task demonstration.

</details>


### [92] [Top-down Traffic Scenario Generation via Joint Initial-Goal Diffusion and Trajectory Infilling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11407)
*Da Saem Lee,Yash Vardhan Pant,Sebastian Fischmeister*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TrafficDiffuser，一种自上而下的交通场景生成框架，通过联合建模初始状态和目标状态对来生成高层交通场景，使初始状态更可解释，并将轨迹生成简化为插值问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通模拟器需要给定智能体的初始状态才能生成轨迹，这限制了可扩展性和多样性；虽然数据驱动的智能体初始化已被广泛研究，但生成的状态缺乏可解释性，且给定初始状态后轨迹生成仍面临目的地可变性和到达方式的挑战。

Method: 提出TrafficDiffuser框架，通过联合建模初始状态和目标状态对来生成高层交通场景（high-level traffic scenarios），将轨迹生成简化为infilling问题。该方法生成的场景可基于不同轨迹模式进行约束，并与现有轨迹生成模型集成。

Result: 在Argoverse 2运动预测数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了生成输出对真实世界分布的捕获能力。除生成目标状态外，TrafficDiffuser在智能体初始化方面优于次优方法，将速度分布距离降低了55.3%，越野率降低了2.8%。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模初始和目标状态，TrafficDiffuser实现了更可解释的场景生成，同时提升了生成的分布保真度，为自动驾驶测试中的交通场景生成提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Robust traffic simulators are crucial for developing and testing autonomous vehicles to reduce the costly, labor-intensive real-world data collection process and the need for physical presence on the road. However, existing simulators require agents' initial states to generate trajectories, which limits scalability and diversity due to restrictions on the given initial states. While data-driven agent initialization has been widely studied, the generated initial states are not interpretable in terms of why the agents are initialized at those specific locations. Given known initial states, trajectory generation is also a challenging problem, as the model must learn the variability of the destination and how agents should reach it over time. In this paper, we propose TrafficDiffuser, a top-down traffic scenario generation framework that generates high-level traffic scenarios, defined by initial and goal state pairs, by jointly modeling them. The high-level scenario generation makes initial states better interpretable and reduces trajectory generation into as simple as an infilling problem. We demonstrate how the generated high-level traffic scenarios can be used, including constraining based on different trajectory modes and integrating them with existing trajectory generation models. We conduct extensive experiments on the Argoverse 2 motion prediction dataset to evaluate how well the generated outputs capture real-world distributions. In addition to generating goal states, TrafficDiffuser outperforms the next-best approach for agent initialization, reducing speed distribution distance by 55.3% and the off-road rate by 2.8%.

</details>


### [93] [From Self-Normal-Positioning to Omni-Directional Tracking: Real-Time Surface Modeling Enabled Probe Tilt Control for Robotic Ultrasound Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11409)
*Xihan Ma,Haichong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向机器人超声系统的全向探头姿态控制框架，融合RGB-D感知、局部表面建模和任务空间姿态控制，能够实时追踪任意非垂直探头角度，解决了传统系统仅支持垂直于体表成像的局限。实验表明平均角度跟踪误差为1.06±0.66度，可恢复高达44.39±2.59度的非垂直倾斜角，并在体模和活体实验中成功获取目标心腔视图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声成像质量高度依赖探头相对患者体表的姿态，而现有机器人超声系统大多局限于探头垂直于局部表面的标准定位方式，这一约束无法满足需要非垂直探头角度的检查（如超声心动图）的临床需求，因此亟需一种能够实时感知局部表面并保持任意期望探头朝向的机器人控制框架。

Method: 该框架由三部分组成：1）RGB-D感知模块用于获取多视角三维点云；2）局部表面建模模块通过融合多视角点云获得局部表面的二次曲面估计；3）任务空间姿态控制模块将期望成像方向相对于表面法线进行编码，使探头能够追踪任意倾斜角度。

Result: 在平面表面跟踪、体模目标角度恢复以及活体专家视图跟踪实验中，系统实现了1.06±0.66度的平均角度跟踪误差，可恢复相对于表面法线最高44.39±2.59度的非垂直倾斜角，并在体模和活体实验中成功获取了期望的心腔视图。

Conclusion: 所提出的全向探头姿态控制框架能够有效支持非垂直探头角度的机器人超声成像，扩展了机器人超声的临床应用范围，为需要特定成像视角的检查（如超声心动图）提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Ultrasound (US) provides real-time, radiation-free imaging, but the image quality depends strongly on how the probe is oriented against the patient body. Robotic US can reduce operator workload and improve acquisition consistency; however, most existing systems focus on normal positioning, where the probe is maintained perpendicular to the local surface. This constraint is inadequate for examinations like echocardiography, where obtaining a diagnostic view requires a non-normal probe angle. Consequently, a clinically useful robotic system must sense the local surface in real-time and preserve the desired probe orientation. Here, we propose an omni-directional probe-orientation control framework that integrates RGB-D perception, local-surface modeling, and task-space orientation control. The surface model fuses multi-view point clouds and provides a quadratic estimate of the local surface. A desired imaging direction is then encoded relative to the normal, enabling the probe to track arbitrary angles. The framework was evaluated through flat-surface tracking, phantom target-angle recovery, and in-vivo tracking of an expert selected view. Results show that the mean angular tracking error was 1.06 +- 0.66 deg. The system recovered a non-normal tilt angle of up to 44.39 +- 2.59 deg relative to the surface normal, and acquired the desired heart chamber view in the phantom and in-vivo experiments.

</details>


### [94] [Locomotion Variability and User Experience in Smart Wheelchair Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11417)
*Sean Kille,Adina M. Panchea,Balint Varga,Sören Hohmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过用户实验研究了共享控制场景中不同辅助策略对人类运动变异性、任务绩效和主观交互体验的影响，提出了一种保留用户自然运动结构的自主支持型共享控制策略，实验表明该策略在保持任务性能的同时显著提升了用户感知能动性和有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类运动本质上具有变异性，且这种变异性根据任务相关性结构化分布。然而在人类-机器人交互（HRI）中，现有的基于模型的辅助策略通常假设人类行为是确定性的，并抑制这种变异性，可能导致交互体验受损、感知能动性下降。虽然运动变异性在功能上日益被认识到其重要性，但在辅助交互中有意保留变异性的影响及其对用户体验的后果仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出一种自主支持型共享控制策略，旨在保留用户自然的运动结构。通过用户实验进行评估：参与者推动智能动力轮椅，在三种条件下进行——无辅助、传统的变异性抑制辅助、以及变异性保留辅助。比较各条件下的任务绩效与主观交互体验。

Result: 辅助模式下任务相关性能相当；与传统辅助相比，保留自然运动变异性带来了更有利的交互体验——参与者报告了显著更高的感知能动性，并且感知有用性最高。

Conclusion: 变异性感知辅助能够在物理人机协作中同时支持性能表现和用户自主性。更广泛地，研究结果强调了辅助机器人系统设计应尊重人类运动的具身结构，而非将变异性视为噪声加以忽视或消除。

Abstract: Human movement is inherently variable, with variability structured according to task relevance: movements are typically more consistent at task-critical points and more flexible elsewhere. In human-robot interaction (HRI), however, model-based assistance strategies commonly assume deterministic human behavior and suppress such variability, potentially altering how interactions are experienced and lowering sense of agency. While movement variability is increasingly recognized as functionally meaningful, its deliberate preservation in assisted interaction, and its consequences for user experience, remain underexplored. In this paper, we empirically investigate how different assistance strategies shape human movement variability, task performance, and subjective interaction experience in a shared control setting. We introduce an autonomy-supportive shared control strategy that preserves users' natural movement structure. This approach is evaluated in a user study in which participants push an intelligent powered wheelchair under three conditions: no assistance, conventional variability-reducing assistance, and variability-preserving assistance. While task-relevant performance remained comparable across assisted modes, preserving natural movement variability led to more favorable interaction experiences. In particular, participants reported significantly higher perceived agency compared to conventional assistance and highest perceived usefulness. These findings suggest that variability-aware assistance can support both performance and user autonomy in physical human-robot collaboration. More broadly, the results highlight the importance of designing assistive robotic systems that respect the embodied structure of human movement rather than treating variability as noise to be neglected or eliminated.

</details>


### [95] [Herding End-to-End Autonomous Driving via Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11451)
*Simón Patiño Idarraga,Erick Silva,Rehana Yasmin,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号安全守卫模块，附着在已训练好的端到端驾驶智能体的最终指令接口上，在执行前用显式安全规则检查指令并替换为最近的安全替代方案，无需重训即可显著减少安全关键碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代端到端驾驶智能体虽然平均性能高，但仍会违反人类驾驶员绝不会犯的基本交通规则。根本原因在于它们在结构上学习的是统计模式而非保证安全驾驶的物理条件，导致决策过程不透明且安全约束无法强制执行。

Method: 提出一个轻量级神经符号安全守卫模块，附着在已训练智能体的最终命令接口上。在命令到达车辆之前，根据显式安全规则检查命令，仅在必要时将其替换为最近的安全替代方案。每次干预都可直接执行且可追溯到触发它的规则，守卫不需要重训练且不增加任何学习组件。

Result: 在长尾基准Fail2Drive和Bench2Drive上使用SOTA模型TransFuser v6(TFv6)作为案例评估，守卫将成功率提高15%，安全关键碰撞减少最多53%，同时保持原有的驾驶分数。

Conclusion: 神经符号安全守卫通过将显式安全规则与神经网络的统计学习相结合，以零训练代价有效弥补了端到端驾驶智能体在安全约束上的结构性缺陷，且每次干预都可解释、可追溯，为安全关键系统提供了实用保障方案。

Abstract: Modern end-to-end driving agents can achieve high average performance yet still violate basic traffic rules that a human driver would never miss. The reason is structural: they learn statistical patterns rather than the physical conditions that guarantee safe driving, leaving their decision-making process opaque and safety constraints unenforced. We introduce a neuro-symbolic safety guard, a lightweight module that attaches to the final command interface of an already-trained agent. Immediately before a command reaches the vehicle, it checks the command against explicit safety rules and, only when necessary, replaces it with the nearest safe alternative. Each intervention is directly executable and traceable to the rule that triggered it, while the guard itself requires no retraining and adds no learned component. Evaluated on the long-tail benchmarks Fail2Drive and Bench2Drive using the state-of-the-art TransFuser v6 (TFv6) as a case study, the guard improves Success Rate by 15% and reduces safety-critical collisions by up to 53%, while preserving the original Driving Score.

</details>


### [96] [Koopman Representation of Nonlinear Virtual Environments in Kinesthetic Haptic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11461)
*Yanting Zhou,Jozsef Kövecses,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Koopman算子表示非线性虚拟环境（VE）并将其融入触觉系统的力反馈渲染，通过仿真和实验验证了其在Duffing振荡器VE上的有效性，并基于该表示开展闭环稳定性分析，相比传统的基于无源性方法保守性更低，且对触觉设备建模不确定性更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在需要高精度力反馈的众多应用中，非线性虚拟环境的触觉渲染至关重要，但传统方法（如基于无源性的稳定性分析）往往过于保守，且非线性模型对触觉设备建模误差敏感，因此需要一种新的表示方式来解决这些问题。

Method: 采用Koopman算子将非线性虚拟环境的动力学提升到线性空间进行表示，使非线性动态以所有提升状态（lifted states）的线性组合形式呈现；基于该表示开展闭环稳定性分析，并设计实验与仿真验证。

Result: 仿真和实验结果均表明该方法能有效表示Duffing振荡器类非线性VE的动力学，多用户研究进一步证实了这一结论；基于Koopman表示的闭环稳定性分析相比传统无源性方法保守性更低，且对触觉设备建模不确定性具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Koopman算子为非线性虚拟环境的触觉渲染提供了一种备选表示方式，既便于进行闭环稳定性分析，又相较于传统非线性模型对建模不确定性更鲁棒，具有较好的应用前景。

Abstract: Rendering haptic feedback with nonlinear virtual environments (VEs) is important in many applications that require highly accurate force feedback. This paper considers the use of the Koopman operator to represent a nonlinear VE interacting with a haptic system. Simulation and experimental results demonstrated that the proposed method provides an effective representation of the nonlinear dynamics of a Duffing-oscillator VE. A multi-user study further confirmed this conclusion. In addition, a closed-loop (CL) stability analysis is performed leveraging the Koopman representation of the nonlinear VE to access stability of the overall haptic system. This alternative way of representing nonlinear VEs enables a convenient CL stability analysis that is less conservative than traditional passivity-based methods. Since a linear combination of all lifted states is used to represent the nonlinearity, such representation is also more robust to uncertainties in the modeling of the haptic device than a traditional nonlinear model.

</details>


### [97] [Keep the Future, Drop the Rollout: RIFT for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11521)
*Chushan Zhang,Jinguang Tong,Xuesong Li,Yikai Wang,Hongdong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RIFT（Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens）方法，通过学习预测token在单次主干网络前向传播中构建完整的未来K/V缓存，替代世界动作模型（WAMs）中成本高昂的迭代视频回滚，在不损失成功率的情况下大幅降低部署延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过在预测的未来上条件化机器人动作来提升决策质量，但迭代式视频回滚（rollout）会显著增加部署延迟。作者提出一个关键问题：动作生成是否真正需要完整的滚动轨迹，还是仅需要其未来表示即可。

Method: 首先通过成对闭环干预实验证明WAMs对未来缓存值及其位置敏感，但部分模型（Joint和Cosmos-2）可复用固定缓存。据此提出RIFT，使用学习到的预期token（anticipation tokens）在单次主干网络前向传播中构建完整的未来K/V缓存，同时保留原始的未来读取接口。

Result: 在LIBERO的40个任务上，RIFT达到98.8%成功率，接近基于回滚的Joint、IDM和LingBot-VA（98.4%-98.6%），同时将动作块延迟降低68.2%-89.1%。在RoboTwin 2.0上，RIFT在干净/随机场景分别达到92.9%/92.6%成功率，为评估方法中最高。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明无需迭代视频生成即可实现无回滚的未来条件化，分离了缓存的消费与生产：模型可以复用固定缓存但需要迭代回滚来构建它，而RIFT通过单次前向传播构建缓存，实现了推理阶段的延迟优化。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) condition robot actions on predicted futures, but iterative video rollout increases deployment latency. We ask whether action generation requires the evolving rollout trajectory or only its future representation. Across four WAMs on all 40 LIBERO tasks, paired closed-loop interventions show that masking or reassigning future-cache values changes execution and reduces success, indicating sensitivity to future values and their assigned positions. For Joint and Cosmos-2, however, replaying one fixed final-clean key/value (K/V) cache nearly preserves unmodified execution, with $1.7$ to $1.9$~cm end-effector average displacement error and $97.9\%$ to $98.2\%$ success. This separates cache consumption from production: these models can reuse a fixed cache but still require iterative rollout to construct it. We therefore propose RIFT (\emph{Rollout-free Imagination via Future Tokens}), which uses learned anticipation tokens to construct a complete future K/V cache in one backbone pass while retaining the original future-read interface. On LIBERO, RIFT achieves $98.8\%$ success, close to rollout-based Joint, IDM, and LingBot-VA at $98.4\%$ to $98.6\%$, while reducing action-chunk latency by $68.2\%$ to $89.1\%$. On RoboTwin~2.0, RIFT reaches $92.9/92.6\%$ on clean/randomized scenes, the highest observed among the evaluated methods. These results support rollout-free future conditioning without iterative video generation at deployment.

</details>


### [98] [RoadWeaver: Large-Scale Lane-Level HD Map Generation from Scratch for Autonomous Driving Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11580)
*Yueyuan Li,Zexi Chen,Weijie Xi,Mingyang Jiang,Songan Zhang,Hanyang Zhuang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoadWeaver 是一个从零生成多样化大规模高清地图的粗到细框架，通过全局路网布局合成、连通路网扩展和车道级几何构建三个阶段，生成可直接部署于自动驾驶仿真器的完整HD地图。实验表明其可达率99.8%、死角率10.7%、端点对齐误差0.24米，相比SOTA方法端点对齐误差降低94.4%，生成时间仅1.39-3.50秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真需要多样化、可扩展的车道级高清地图来支撑长时程评估，但现有方法要么依赖人工构建或重建真实地图导致扩展性受限，要么只能生成局部道路结构而非完整高清地图，难以满足大规模仿真需求。

Method: RoadWeaver采用粗到细的三阶段框架：首先合成全局道路布局，其次将其扩展为连通的道路网络，最后构建具有拓扑一致车道连通性的车道级几何结构。

Result: RoadWeaver实现99.8%的可达率、10.7%的死角率和0.24米的端点对齐误差；相比SOTA生成方法将端点对齐误差降低94.4%，并能以1.39-3.50秒的速度生成完整高清地图，可直接部署于驾驶仿真器。

Conclusion: RoadWeaver有效解决了从零生成大规模多样化高清地图的难题，生成的图可直接部署于自动驾驶仿真器，为未来自动驾驶系统的闭环评估提供了可扩展的仿真环境。

Abstract: Autonomous driving simulation requires diverse and scalable lane-level HD maps to support long-horizon evaluation across complex road networks. Existing approaches either rely on handcrafted or reconstructed real-world maps, which limits scalability, or generate only local road structures rather than complete HD maps. We present RoadWeaver, a coarse-to-fine framework for from-scratch generation of diverse, large-scale HD maps. RoadWeaver first synthesizes a global road layout, expands it into a connected road network, and then constructs lane-level geometry with topologically consistent lane connectivity. Experimental results show that RoadWeaver achieves a 99.8\% reachability, a 10.7\% dead-end ratio, and an endpoint alignment error of 0.24 m. Compared with SOTA generation methods, it reduces endpoint alignment error by 94.4\% while generating complete HD maps in 1.39--3.50 s. The generated maps can be directly deployed in driving simulators, providing scalable simulation environments for future closed-loop evaluation of autonomous driving systems. The training code and an out-of-the-box implementation of RoadWeaver will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [99] [Video2Track: From Real-World Interaction Videos to Steerable Adversarial Closed-Track Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11592)
*Mengjie Tian,Xinrui Zhang,Tianyu Li,Peizhi Zhang,Guirong Zhou,Haojie Feng,Junpeng Huang,Qixiang Zhang,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Video2Track框架，将真实世界的交互式驾驶场景从视频迁移到可控的封闭赛道对抗性测试中，通过视觉语言模型提取语义并结合检索增强生成映射到赛道拓扑，再利用条件扩散模型和Stackelberg博弈生成可控风险与交互风格的多智能体轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶封闭赛道测试大多依赖标准化协议或预定义轨迹，导致交互过于脚本化，无法复现真实道路驾驶中自然的复杂交互行为，缺乏真实性和可操控性。

Method: Video2Track由两个紧密耦合模块组成：(1) 场景语义映射模块，利用视觉语言模型从驾驶视频中提取结构化语义，并通过检索增强生成将其映射到封闭赛道拓扑库，识别兼容的地图片段和交互锚点；(2) 动态交互测试模块，基于映射的拓扑和锚点，通过条件扩散模型生成多样化的多智能体轨迹，并使用带参数化对抗目标的Stackelberg博弈调控交互强度。

Result: 封闭赛道实验表明，该框架能够忠实地复现具有代表性的真实世界交互场景，并生成具有可控风险等级和交互风格的可执行场景变体，为真实且可操控的自动驾驶系统验证提供了可扩展的方法。

Conclusion: Video2Track提供了一种从真实视频到封闭赛道测试的可扩展迁移方案，兼顾真实性与可操控性，弥补了现有封闭赛道测试方法在自然交互复杂性和灵活调控方面的不足。

Abstract: Closed-track testing plays a fundamental role in the verification and validation of automated driving systems (ADS), particularly for safety-critical scenarios, by enabling reproducible evaluation under controlled conditions. However, most existing approaches still rely on standardized protocols or predefined trajectories, leading to overly scripted interactions and limited ability to reproduce the natural complexity of public-road traffic. To address this limitation, we propose Video2Track, a framework that transfers real-world interactive driving scenarios from videos into steerable adversarial closed-track testing. The framework consists of two tightly coupled modules. The first is a scenario semantic mapping module, which extracts structured semantics from driving videos using a vision-language model and grounds them onto a closed-track topology library via retrieval-augmented generation, thereby identifying compatible map segments and interaction anchors. The second is a dynamic interactive testing module, which conditions on the grounded topology and anchors to generate diverse multi-agent trajectories through a conditional diffusion model, while regulating interaction intensity via a Stackelberg game with a parameterized adversarial objective. Closed-track experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework can faithfully reproduce representative real-world interaction scenarios and generate executable scenario variants with controllable risk levels and interaction styles, providing a scalable approach for realistic and steerable ADS validation.

</details>


### [100] [IoT-Enabled Autonomous Maritime Navigation in Smart Ports: A Curriculum-Guided Shared Policy Learning Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11597)
*Yuqing Lin,Rangya Zhang,Kum Fai Yuen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究智能港口中物联网自主海事设备在部分可观测与高密度交通条件下的船载自主导航问题，提出一种课程引导的强化学习框架，采用共享循环策略增强时序推理、部署可扩展性和边缘决策鲁棒性。集中训练作为离线设计策略，所有导航动作在船端执行，符合IoT边缘智能范式。仿真表明该方法在导航可靠性、碰撞避免和训练稳定性方面优于基线，并能泛化到未见过的密集场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能港口基础设施依赖配备物联网的自主海事设备，但在航道拥堵和部分可观测条件下，确保可靠的船载导航智能成为安全、可扩展运营的关键挑战。

Method: 提出课程引导的强化学习（curriculum-guided reinforcement learning）框架，搭配共享循环策略（shared recurrent policy）以增强时序推理、部署可扩展性和鲁棒性。采用集中训练（centralized training）作为离线设计策略，全部导航动作在船端执行（onboard execution），契合IoT边缘智能范式。

Result: 在多个逼真港口环境的广泛仿真中，所提方法在导航可靠性、碰撞避免和训练稳定性方面均优于标准基线方法，并能有效泛化到之前未见的高密度场景。

Conclusion: 课程引导的共享学习为智能港口中IoT使能自主海事设备的可扩展部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: As smart port infrastructures increasingly rely on autonomous maritime devices enabled by the Internet of Things (IoT), ensuring reliable onboard navigation intelligence has become a critical challenge for safe and scalable operations in congested waterways. This paper investigates onboard autonomous navigation for such IoT devices under partial observability and dense traffic conditions. A curriculum-guided reinforcement learning framework with a shared recurrent policy is developed to enhance temporal reasoning, deployment scalability, and robustness of edge-level decision-making. Centralized training is adopted as an offline design-time strategy, while all navigation actions are executed fully onboard, consistent with IoT edge intelligence paradigms. Extensive simulations in multiple realistic port environments demonstrate that the proposed approach improves navigation reliability, collision avoidance, and training stability compared with standard baseline methods, and generalizes effectively to previously unseen high-density scenarios. The results indicate that curriculum-guided shared learning provides a practical solution for scalable deployment of IoT-enabled autonomous maritime devices in smart port operations.

</details>


### [101] [StellaVLA: In-Context Structured Demonstration for Generalizable Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11671)
*Siyu Xu,Yunke Wang,Zijian Wang,Dihao Zhu,Chenghao Xia,Chengbin Du,Daochang Liu,Tao Huang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StellaVLA是一个通过检索单个演示并在测试时进行上下文条件化的VLA框架，将原始轨迹离线转化为结构化演示（任务计划、子目标描述、3D运动语言化），使策略在测试时不依赖模仿像素轨迹而是理解任务意图，实现零标注的跨实体泛化和OOD适应。在VLA-Arena排行榜上位列第一（0.63分），并在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus基准上分别达到98.8%和85.1%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽然能遵循指令并操作物体，但在分布外（OOD）场景（如场景、视角或物体变化）下性能会大幅下降。传统解决方案需要收集更多数据并微调，成本高昂且不具实时性，因此需要一种无需额外数据收集的测试时自适应方法。

Method: StellaVLA的核心方法是：(1) 通过自动离线pipeline将原始轨迹转化为结构化演示（任务计划、子目标描述、语言化的3D运动），零人工标注成本；(2) 将结构化演示作为上下文指导提供给策略，使策略推理任务意图而非模仿像素轨迹；(3) 采用并行双训练设计，通过联合动作-语言目标在训练中内化这种推理能力，推理时仅使用动作专家以保持实时高频控制，不增加延迟。该方法天然支持跨实体迁移（真实机器人、人手、XR演示）。

Result: 在VLA-Arena排行榜（2026年8月1日）上以0.63的总体分排名第一，远超强基线模型π0.5（0.44）和LingBot-VLA（0.22）；在LIBERO上达到98.8%的平均成功率，在LIBERO-Plus上达到85.1%的成功率。真实机器人基准测试验证了其能利用人类/机器人演示及人性到机器人的XR演示作为上下文结构化演示，帮助VLA模型适应OOD任务。

Conclusion: StellaVLA通过将演示从"模仿行为"提升为"理解意图"，以结构化演示作为上下文条件，实现了无需微调的测试时OOD适应，同时保持推理阶段的实时性和跨实体可迁移性，为VLA模型的泛化与快速适配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can follow instructions and manipulate objects, but their performance often collapses out of distribution (OOD), when the scene, viewpoint, or object differs from training. Adapting to each new situation typically requires collecting more data and fine-tuning. We present StellaVLA, a framework that instead adapts at test time by conditioning on a single retrieved demonstration. The key idea is to move beyond imitating what an expert did and instead convey why: an automated offline pipeline converts each raw trajectory into a structured demonstration, e.g., a task plan, sub-goal descriptions, and verbalized 3D motion, at zero human-annotation cost. Provided as in-context guidance, this structured demonstration lets the policy reason about the task rather than mimic a pixel trajectory, which also makes it transferable across embodiments (real-robot, human-hand, or XR demonstrations). A parallel dual-training design internalizes this reasoning during training through a joint action-and-language objective, while inference uses the action expert alone, preserving real-time, high-frequency control with no added latency. On the VLA-Arena leaderboard(Aug 1, 2026), StellaVLA ranks first with an overall score of 0.63, versus 0.44 and 0.22 for the strong prior models ($π_{0.5}$ and LingBot-VLA), and it further leads on LIBERO with 98.8% average success rate and LIBERO-Plus with 85.1% success rate. Our real-robot benchmark demonstrates that StellaVLA can use both human/robot demos and human-to-robot (XR) demos as in-context structured demonstration to help VLA model adapt to OOD tasks.

</details>


### [102] [ContactIPM: A Structure-Exploiting Interior-Point Solver for Contact-Implicit Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11731)
*Yucheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ContactIPM 是一种面向接触隐式轨迹优化的原对偶内点求解器，通过障碍耦合的弹性内点松弛处理互补性约束，结合逐阶段消元与Riccati递归求解约化牛顿系统，并采用固定多阶段MPCC恢复调度与未松弛互补性残差门控终止准则，在多项基准测试中相比CRISP和IMPACT实现了显著加速（最高8.87倍）并提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触隐式轨迹优化无需预先规定接触序列，但产生的带互补约束的数学规划（MPCC）其退化性给传统原对偶求解器带来挑战。已有的接触专用方法虽提高了对退化的鲁棒性，但未能利用分阶段最优控制因子分解和原对偶一致性；而利用结构的最优控制求解器又不面向互补约束设计。因此需要一个能同时结合这两方面能力的方法。

Method: ContactIPM包含以下核心组件：(1) 识别互补不等式对；(2) 通过障碍耦合的弹性内点松弛将它们嵌入；(3) 分阶段消去松弛变量和对偶变量；(4) 使用Riccati递归求解约化的牛顿系统；(5) 固定多阶段MPCC恢复调度提供四次延续和重启尝试（支持朴素初始化）；(6) 以未松弛的物理互补性残差作为终止门控。

Result: 在四个固定CRISP基准测试案例中，ContactIPM比CRISP快2.17到8.87倍（每案例20次配对计时重复），并在Push Box和Push-T鲁棒性套件上取得更高成功率。在IMpact对比中，Push T上快2.96倍，Cart Transport上快4.91倍，但Push Box上慢4.46倍。在50次包含模型失配、测量噪声、初始位姿误差和状态重置的闭环Push Box滚动测试中（结果未完整给出）。

Conclusion: ContactIPM证明了分阶段最优控制因子分解与原对偶一致性可以与互补约束处理能力在同一原对偶方法中成功结合，在多数基准上相对现有的接触专用MPCC求解器（CRISP和IMPACT）具有显著的求解速度和鲁棒性优势，但Push Box案例上相对IMPACT仍有性能差距，提示该方法在不同问题类型上的适用性存在差异。

Abstract: Contact-implicit trajectory optimization avoids prescribing contact sequences, but yields mathematical programs with complementarity constraints (MPCCs) whose degeneracy challenges conventional primal--dual solvers. Existing contact-specific methods improve robustness to this degeneracy but do not leverage a stagewise optimal-control factorization and primal--dual consistency, while structure-exploiting optimal-control solvers are not designed for complementarity constraints. We show that these capabilities can be combined in a single primal--dual method. ContactIPM identifies complementary inequality pairs, embeds them through a barrier-coupled elastic interior relaxation, eliminates slack and dual variables stagewise, and solves the reduced Newton system using a Riccati recursion. A fixed multi-phase MPCC recovery schedule provides four continuation and restart attempts from naive initializations, while termination is gated by the unrelaxed physical complementarity residual.
  We compare ContactIPM with two contact-specific MPCC solvers, CRISP and IMPACT, using matched benchmark conditions and common post-solve acceptance criteria. On four fixed CRISP benchmark cases, ContactIPM is $2.17$--$8.87\times$ faster over 20 paired timing repetitions per case and achieves higher success on the Push Box and Push-T robustness suites. Against IMPACT, ContactIPM is \(2.96\times\) faster on Push T and \(4.91\times\) faster on Cart Transport, but \(4.46\times\) slower on Push Box. In 50 closed-loop Push Box rollouts spanning model mismatch, measurement noise, initial-pose errors, and state resets,

</details>


### [103] [G0.5: One Autoregressive Stream for Robot Reasoning and Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11739)
*Yicheng Liu,Zibin Dong,Baijun Ye,Tianyuan Yuan,Tao Jiang,Anqi Yang,Shicheng Cao,Haonan Liu,Yue Sun,Zihan Guo,Xiao Liu,Dong Ke,Changxun Pan,Chenru Wu,Tailai Cheng,Xiaoshu Ren,Xinlei Zhang,Jianning Cui,Zijie Zhao,Haoyu Zhang,Kaiming Xu,Haodong Yang,Bowen Zhang,Jiahui Niu,Shaoting Zhu,Shiduo Zhang,Hang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出G0.5，一种预训练的自回归视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过单一Transformer解码器在统一目标下同时生成推理token和动作token，无需分离的动作专家模块。其核心创新包括可学习的跨具身动作分词器、原生思维链流和视觉记忆模块，使预训练VLM的推理能力直接迁移到物理行为控制，在7个独立评测基准上全面超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的主流范式是将预训练VLM作为上下文编码器，与单独训练的流匹配动作专家解耦，导致VLM并未真正参与决策。作者希望打破这一局限，让VLM本身成为决策者，实现推理与行动的深度融合，从而将VLM强大的语言理解和泛化能力直接迁移到机器人动作生成中。

Method: G0.5由三个关键组件构成：（1）可学习的跨具身动作分词器，将异构机器人动作映射为共享词汇表；（2）原生思维链流，在动作token间交错插入任务分解、目标定位和动作提示；（3）视觉记忆模块，通过视觉编码器注入多秒历史信息。模型以单一自回归Transformer解码器在单一训练目标下联合生成推理和动作token，并在大规模机器人数据和VQA样本上联合预训练。

Result: G0.5在7个独立评测基准上超越现有SOTA：真实机器人微调（R1lite/R1pro达76.7%，远超π0.5的53.3%和GR00T-N1.7的24.4%）、2025 BEHAVIOR挑战赛50个长时程家务移动操作任务（31.4% vs 挑战冠军26.1%）、DROID后训练零样本迁移（82.5%）、LIBERO语言跟随Pick-and-Place（98.9%）、RoboTwin 2.0（93.3%）、SimplerEnv-Bridge（87.3%）。

Conclusion: 该工作证明将推理和动作融合到单一自回归模型中是可行且可扩展的，实现了推理能力到物理行为的直接迁移。无需额外训练，用户只需通过prompt即可操控动作粒度、任务时程和分布外场景处理，标志着VLA从上下文编码范式向真决策者范式的转变。

Abstract: The prevailing recipe for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models couples a pretrained VLM with a separately trained flow-matching action expert. This makes the VLM a context encoder rather than a decision-maker. We introduce G0.5, a pretrained autoregressive VLA in which a single transformer decoder emits reasoning and action tokens under a single objective. Three components make this tractable at foundation-model scale: a learnable cross-embodiment action tokenizer that maps heterogeneous robot actions into a shared vocabulary; a native chain-of-thought stream interleaving task decomposition, object grounding, and action hints with action tokens; and a visual memory module that injects multi-second history through the vision encoder. Because reasoning and action share a single set of weights, the pretrained VLM's capabilities carry over to physical behavior: the model follows instructions closely, and prompts directly steer action granularity, task horizon, and out-of-distribution scene handling without further training. Pretrained on a large collection of robot datasets together with VQA samples, G0.5 surpasses state-of-the-art models across 7 independent regimes: real-world fine-tuning on R1lite and R1pro robots (76.7\% vs.\ 53.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 24.4\% for GR00T-N1.7), the 2025 BEHAVIOR Challenge on 50 long-horizon household mobile manipulation tasks using a generalist policy (31.4\% vs.\ 26.3\% for $π_{0.5}$ and 26.1\% for the challenge winner), DROID post-training followed by zero-shot transfer to an unseen environment and objects (82.5\%), a language-following Pick-and-Place benchmark, LIBERO (98.9\%), RoboTwin 2.0 (93.3\%), and SimplerEnv-Bridge (87.3\%).

</details>


### [104] [Policy-Induced Hand Priors in Humanoid Dual-Arm Manipulation: Diagnosing and Mitigating Initial-Pose Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11769)
*Chaeyeon Jung,Juyoun Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究VLA策略在类人双臂操作中对初始姿态的依赖性，发现存在"策略诱导的手部先验"（policy-induced hand prior），通过HandPriorScore、残差手偏置、目标响应度等指标量化，并揭示数据覆盖和训练构成对初始姿态鲁棒性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略预期在不同机器人初始配置下稳健运行，但聚合任务成功率会掩盖特定姿态下的失败以及不合适的手部选择。作者希望深入探究初始姿态对VLA类人双臂操作性能的影响，而非仅依赖总体成功率评估。

Method: 提出HandPriorScore、残差手偏置（residual hand bias）和目标响应度（target responsiveness）三个量化指标，评估多种策略在17种初始配置下的性能，分析初始姿态与策略的交互作用，并通过扩展训练数据初始姿态覆盖范围、针对性增强低性能配置等方式验证鲁棒性改进。

Result: 同一姿态在不同策略间成功率差异显著，同一策略在不同姿态下性能波动大；特定初始手臂配置可抑制或诱发不对称手偏好，方向和强度随策略而异；腕部相机观测影响手选择和任务性能；扩展初始姿态覆盖显著提升鲁棒性，针对性增强提升低性能配置成功率；训练构成（仿真比例、姿势覆盖、观测可用性）对结果有不同影响。

Conclusion: 本文刻画了姿态条件化的手部先验，识别出局部初始手臂配置作为手选择行为的因果控制手柄，并证明数据覆盖与训练构成对初始姿态鲁棒性具有显著影响。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies are expected to operate robustly across variations in the robot's initial configuration, yet aggregate task success can conceal pose-specific failures and inappropriate hand selection. This work investigates initial-pose dependence in VLA-based humanoid dual-arm manipulation. We characterize the initial-condition-dependent early hand preference as a policy-induced hand prior and quantify it using HandPriorScore, residual hand bias, and target responsiveness. Evaluations across multiple policies and 17 initial configurations reveal strong initial-pose--policy interactions: the same pose produces substantially different success rates across policies, while a single policy exhibits large performance variation across poses. Specific initial arm configurations can suppress or induce an asymmetric hand preference, with the resulting effect varying in direction and strength across policies. Wrist-camera observations also influence hand selection and task performance. Expanding initial-pose coverage in the training dataset substantially improves robustness, while targeted augmentation around a low-performing configuration increases its success rate. Comparisons across training configurations show that sufficient exposure to the target simulation task is beneficial, whereas the effect of real or auxiliary data depends on pose coverage, simulation ratio, and observation availability. These findings characterize a pose-conditioned hand prior, identify a localized initial arm configuration as a causal handle on hand-selection behavior, and demonstrate how data coverage and training composition affect initial-pose robustness.

</details>


### [105] [Enhancing Visual Domain Robustness in Behaviour Cloning via Saliency-Guided Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11870)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nils Ingelhag,Ville Kyrki,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboSaGA，一种基于显著性引导的叠加式数据增强方法，用于视觉行为克隆。它通过策略驱动的显著性在像素级别动态调整增强强度，在任务无关区域进行激进增强，同时保留任务关键信息，无需修改网络结构或增加训练目标。实验证明其在模拟和真实环境中均能保持域内性能，同时显著提升对视觉域偏移（光照、阴影、干扰物、背景变化）的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉行为克隆中，传统图像增强（如随机裁剪、颜色抖动）在面对显著的视觉域偏移（阴影、干扰物、背景变化）时往往效果不佳。虽然叠加式增强在计算机视觉中展现了对泛化能力的提升潜力，但在行为克隆中，任务关键语义、时空关系以及智能体-目标交互必须被保留，这使得叠加式增强的适用性存在疑问。

Method: RoboSaGA属于叠加式增强家族，利用策略驱动的显著性图在像素级别动态调整增强强度。具体而言，它根据策略网络产生的显著性信息识别任务关键区域和非关键区域，在非关键区域施加更激进的增强，同时保护任务关键信息不被破坏。该方法无需对现有网络结构进行修改，也不引入额外的学习目标，可直接无缝集成到现有架构中。

Result: 在模拟环境和真实世界实验中，RoboSaGA在保持域内性能的同时，显著提升了模型对视觉域偏移的鲁棒性，包括干扰物变化、背景变化、光照变化和阴影变化等场景。

Conclusion: RoboSaGA为视觉行为克隆提供了一种有效的、即插即用的数据增强方案，在不牺牲域内性能的前提下大幅增强对视觉域偏移的泛化能力，同时保持了方法集成的高兼容性。

Abstract: In vision-based behavior cloning (BC), conventional image augmentations such as Random Crop and Color Jitter often fall short under substantial visual domain shifts, including changes in shadows, distractors, and backgrounds. Superimposition-based augmentations, which blend in-domain and out-of-domain images, have shown promise for improving generalization in computer vision, but their suitability for BC remains uncertain because task-critical semantics, spatiotemporal relationships, and agent-target interactions must be preserved. To address this, we introduce RoboSaGA, a Saliency-Guided Augmentation method within the superimposition family tailored for vision-based BC. RoboSaGA dynamically adjusts augmentation intensity at the pixel level using policy-driven saliency, enabling aggressive augmentation in task-irrelevant regions while preserving task-critical information. It integrates seamlessly into existing architectures without requiring structural modifications or additional learning objectives. Experiments in both simulated and real-world settings show that RoboSaGA preserves in-domain performance while substantially improving robustness to visual domain shifts, including distractor and background changes, as well as lighting and shadow variations. Code is available at https://github.com/Zheyu-Zhuang/RoboSaGA.

</details>


### [106] [D3D-GEN: Robot-Aware Domain-Grounded Interactive 3D World Generation for Social Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11876)
*Anh Duc Do,Volodymyr Scherbyna,Tai Duc Nguyen,Spaarsh Thakkar,Zhengcheng Shen,Teham Buiyan,Archan Misra,Linh Kästner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: D3D-GEN是一个结合领域智能体与检索增强生成(RAG)的世界生成系统，能够快速生成领域约束、完全可交互的3D仿真世界，无需依赖固定3D模型数据库，并支持Isaac Sim和Gazebo等主流仿真器加载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交导航的具身AI训练与验证严重依赖真实感的仿真环境，但现有方法难以在真实性与可仿真性之间取得平衡，缺乏兼顾两者且高效的3D世界生成方案。

Method: 系统由两部分组成：1) 研究智能体自动收集公开领域数据并构建持久化领域数据库；2) 基于该数据库的RAG管线通过动态查询用户提供的语义数据库来生成合理的平面布局和物体摆放，最终输出可交互3D世界。

Result: 系统已为室内住宅、医院、办公室等多个常见领域构建数据库，并为每个领域生成数十个不同且合理的仿真环境，同时提供本地Web前端支持交互式快速世界生成。

Conclusion: D3D-GEN验证了将领域智能体与RAG管线相结合可有效实现领域约束下的3D仿真世界自动生成，在无需固定模型库的前提下平衡了仿真环境的真实性与可仿真性。

Abstract: Training and validation of Embodied AI for social navigation critically depends on realistic simulation environments, yet many current approaches fail to find a balance between realism and simulability. We propose D3D-GEN, a novel world generation system that combines a domain agent with a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline grounded in that domain. Our system enables users to rapidly generate domain-grounded, fully interactive 3D worlds by automating both the collection of domain knowledge and the synthesis of realistic floorplans and object placements, without dependence on any fixed 3D model database. Given a domain description prompt, the research agent collects publicly accessible domain-specific data and constructs a persistent domain database. Using this database, our RAG pipeline generates plausible floorplans and object placements by dynamically querying a user-provided semantic database, which can be easily extended or modified. The output is a fully interactive 3D world loadable by the popular simulators Isaac Sim and Gazebo. With our approach, we have built databases for several common domains (indoor residential, hospital, office) and generated dozens of distinct, plausible simulation environments for each domain. We present D3D-GEN with a local web frontend that facilitates rapid, interactive world generation for robot simulation.

</details>


### [107] [Scalable Multi-Agent Maze Traversal with Local Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11895)
*Julian Rau,Jahir Argote-Gerald,Grace McFassel,Genki Miyauchi,Paul Trodden,Roderich Groß*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分布式多智能体导航算法，用于未知且可能含环的图中集体探索与目标定位。算法通过领导者-跟随者关系和领导者切换实现协作，任一时刻仅一个智能体执行单智能体迷宫求解器进行探索。作者证明了算法完备性、makespan渐进最优性，并推导了时间和空间复杂度。仿真表明随着智能体数量增加，平均总燃料消耗下降，且优于所有智能体独立执行单智能体求解器的朴素基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洞穴网络、管道系统等迷宫类环境对多智能体在未知场景且通信受限条件下的导航提出重大挑战。现有单智能体求解器无法直接扩展，而多智能体导航需要解决碰撞避免、任务分配和协作策略等问题。本文旨在设计一个分布式算法，使智能体在未知可能有环的图中集体完成从起点到未公开目标点的导航任务。

Method: 提出分布式算法：智能体按顺序从指定起点进入，通过局部通信和领导者-跟随者关系及领导者切换实现协调。任意时刻仅由单一智能体执行探索任务，该智能体运行单智能体迷宫求解器。其余跟随者沿已建立的路径推进，避免碰撞。算法在理论上证明了完备性、与最优全知策略渐近等价的makespan，并推导时间和空间复杂度。

Result: 仿真实验使用最多625个智能体，结果显示平均sum-of-fuels（总燃料消耗）随智能体数量增加而降低，表明协作扩展有效分摊了探索成本；同时所提方法优于所有智能体独立执行单智能体求解器的朴素基线方案。

Conclusion: 该分布式算法能够在通信受限的未知环图中实现多智能体高效协作导航，具有完备性和接近最优的时间性能，且随智能体数量增加展现出良好的可扩展性和资源效率。

Abstract: Cave networks, pipe systems, and similar maze-like environments pose significant challenges for multi-agent navigation in unknown settings with limited communication. We propose a distributed algorithm that enables agents to collectively traverse an unknown, possibly cyclic graph. Agents enter sequentially at a designated start node and are tasked to localize and reach an undisclosed goal while avoiding collisions. They coordinate via local communication using leader-follower relationships and leader switching. At any moment in time, exploration is performed by only one of the agents, which runs a single-agent maze solver. We prove that the algorithm is complete, that its makespan is asymptotically equivalent (in the number of agents) to that of an optimal full-knowledge strategy, and derive its time and space complexity. Simulations with up to $625$ agents show a decreasing average sum-of-fuels as the number of agents increases and demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms a naïve baseline in which all agents independently execute the single-agent solver.

</details>


### [108] [DaViNCi: A Dataset Towards Outdoor Vision-and-Language Navigation with Continuous Actions and Dynamic Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11901)
*Zihao Xie,Pingrui Lai,Yitong Wu,Hua Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DaViNCi，这是首个同时引入连续与动态因素的室外视觉语言导航（VLN）数据集，包含6个地图、6,933条轨迹，通过在连续环境中进行动作并处理动态元素来弥合仿真与现实之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室外VLN数据集依赖固定离散拓扑图构建，无法与快速变化的真实室外环境对齐，且阻碍VLN智能体从仿真到现实的迁移。

Method: 构建DaViNCi数据集，同时引入连续行动空间与不可预测的动态元素；智能体既需要以连续动作在室外环境移动，又需应对动态干扰；基于该数据集进行多组对比实验。

Result: 在DaViNCi上，离散环境的成功率相比此前数据集下降超过10%，连续环境下降更为显著，验证了任务难度；实验结果还揭示了动作粒度与动态元素对性能的影响。

Conclusion: DaViNCi证实了连续与动态因素对室外VLN的挑战性，证明了其在推动室外VLN向更真实环境发展方面的实用价值。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) has progressively expanded from indoor to outdoor environments. However, existing outdoor VLN datasets still rely on fixed discrete topological graphs for construction. It fails to align with the rapidly changing real-world outdoor environments and impedes the sim-to-real transfer of VLN agents. To address this limitation, we propose DaViNCi (\textbf{D}yn\textbf{a}mic \textbf{Vi}sion-and-Language \textbf{N}avigation in \textbf{C}ont\textbf{i}nuous Environment), the first outdoor VLN dataset that simultaneously introduces both continuous and dynamic factors. The agent not only moves in the outdoor environment using continuous actions but is also required to handle unpredictable dynamic elements. The dataset encompasses six distinct maps with a total of 6,933 trajectories. Through comprehensive comparative experiments, we find that the success rate on DaViNCi decreased by more than 10\% in discrete environments compared to previous datasets. And there is an even greater decline in continuous settings, demonstrating the challenge of DaViNCi. Furthermore, we clarify the impact of action granularity and dynamic elements. These results demonstrate the practical value of DaViNCi in advancing outdoor VLN toward more realistic environments. The website is https://xzh0312.github.io/DaViNCi/.

</details>


### [109] [Learning Loco-Manipulation From SMPC Demonstrations With Sparse Offline-to-Online RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12063)
*Martin Schuck,Maks Sorokin,Simone Manni,Duy Ta,Angela P. Schoellig,Marco Hutter,Simon Le Cleac'H,Jan Brüdigam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用纯仿真中的基于采样的模型预测控制（SMPC）作为自动化专家，生成大规模离线数据集，从而用稀疏任务奖励训练离策略强化学习代理，并集成低层动态稳定性控制器，实现超越专家教师的复杂移动操控技能，并在Spot四足机器人和G1人形机器人上验证了sim-to-real的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习在复杂任务上受限于密集奖励整定的缓慢手动过程，严重制约了机器人自主性中移动与操控整合的扩展。

Method: 在仿真中使用纯基于采样的模型预测控制（SMPC）作为可快速调节的自动化专家生成大规模离线数据，解决探索问题；随后仅用稀疏任务奖励训练离策略RL代理，并将其与低层动态稳定性控制器集成。

Result: 学习到的策略超越了原始最优控制教师，并成功在不同形态的机器人（带臂Spot四足和G1人形）上部署了复杂的移动操控技能。

Conclusion: 该sim-to-real框架通过自动化教师数据生成替代人工奖励设计，大幅减少了学习新技能所需时间，消除了手动调参需求，并实现了与真实任务目标严格对齐的更优行为。

Abstract: Integrating locomotion and manipulation is essential for robot autonomy, but scaling standard Reinforcement Learning (RL) to complex tasks is severely bottlenecked by the slow, manual process of dense reward shaping. To bypass this limitation, we leverage Sample-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) entirely in simulation as an automated, rapidly tunable expert to generate massive offline datasets. Because this data solves the fundamental exploration problem, we can train an off-policy RL agent using purely sparse task rewards, drastically reducing the time required to learn new skills and eliminating the need for manual tuning. Integrating this high-level agent with a low-level dynamic stability controller yields more optimal behaviors that strictly align with true task objectives, ultimately allowing the learned policies to surpass the original optimal control teacher. We validate the robustness of this sim-to-real framework by successfully deploying complex loco-manipulation skills across different morphologies, including an arm-equipped Spot quadruped and a G1 humanoid.

</details>


### [110] [HandEdit: A Unified Benchmark for Egocentric Human-to-Robot Dexterous Hand Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12122)
*Zhenjie Yang,Xingyu Jiao,Guopeng Zhong,Shuzhe Yang,Shi Che,Chao Wu,Chenyu Jiang,Dongjie Zhang,Yideng Zhang,Zheng Zhang,Muyun Jiang,Haisheng Su,Shuang Jin,Donghang Zhang,Chao Yang,Li Chen,Hongyang Li,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HandEdit是一个大规模具身感知图像编辑数据集与基准，将第一人称视频中的人手/手臂转换为多种灵巧机器人形态，包含超过2亿个编辑实例、26种不同URDF配置，并建立了双轨评估协议，旨在弥合人类与机器人数据之间的差距，助力可扩展的具身智能灵巧操作学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手的机器人操作是具身智能的核心，但遥操作数据的收集成本高昂。虽然第一人称人类手部视频是廉价且可扩展的替代方案，但人类与机器人外观、关节运动和相机视角之间的显著差异使得跨域联合训练面临巨大挑战。

Method: 构建HandEdit数据集，涵盖5个不同来源数据集、200M+编辑实例、26种URDF（13种仅手部+13种手-臂组合）；建立统一基准协议，包含Hand-only和Hand-Arm两个评测轨道，支持URDF条件化评估；采用多维指标套件（通用相似度指标、VLM评判、具身感知指标）对11个代表性图像编辑基线进行全面评测。

Result: 对11个代表性图像编辑基线进行了全面评估，结果揭示了现有通用图像编辑模型在具身感知编辑任务上的不足，展示了HandEdit作为推动具身感知编辑模型发展关键资源的价值。

Conclusion: HandEdit填补了图像编辑与机器人学交叉领域的关键空白，能够推动具身感知编辑模型的发展，并利用海量人类视频数据实现可扩展的灵巧机器人学习，为更泛化的具身智能铺平道路。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation with dexterous hands is a cornerstone of Embodied AI, yet its progress is stifled by the high cost of collecting embodiment-aware teleoperation data. While abundant egocentric videos of human hands offer a scalable alternative, the profound discrepancies in appearance, articulation, and camera viewpoints between human and robotic data raise significant challenges for co-training. Though existing general image-editing models demonstrate strong capabilities, they lack necessary embodiment-specific priors to fully bridge this gap. In this work, we present HandEdit, a unified large-scale embodiment-aware image-editing dataset and benchmark specifically designed to transform human hands and arms into various dexterous robotic embodiments within egocentric frames. HandEdit comprises over 200M editing instances derived from five diverse source datasets, covering 26 distinct URDFs, including 13 hand-only and 13 hand-arm configurations. Alongside the dataset, we establish a unified benchmark protocol with two tracks: Hand-only and Hand-Arm, supporting URDF-conditioned evaluation. We conduct extensive evaluations of 11 representative image-editing baselines using a multi-dimensional metric suite, including generic similarity metrics, VLM-based judgment, and embodiment-aware metrics. HandEdit serves as a critical resource at the intersection of image editing and robotics: it advances embodiment-aware editing models while enabling scalable dexterous robotic learning from abundant human video data, paving the way for more generalizable Embodied AI.

</details>


### [111] [Learning-Based Behavior Planning for Automated Driving: Real-World Integration and Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12198)
*Jean-Pierre Busch,Guido Linden,Jan Bergmann,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合规划架构，将深度学习模型（用于解释复杂交通场景并生成驾驶行为提议）与基于优化的监督层（用于验证提议并强制执行可驾驶性和安全约束）相结合，在真实城市数据集上进行了开环评估和实车部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的运动规划方法虽在复杂环境中展现出提升自动驾驶车辆驾驶行为的潜力，但其复杂性和缺乏透明度阻碍了可解释性和可信度，也使得安全保证变得困难。

Method: 提出混合规划架构：(1) 深度神经网络用于解读复杂交通场景并生成驾驶行为提议；(2) 基于优化的监督层验证该提议并强制执行明确的可驾驶性和安全约束，从而结合机器学习优势与传统方法的可验证性和确定性。

Result: 在真实城市数据上进行了开环研究评估学习的规划器驾驶行为，讨论了闭环稳定运行的系统集成方面，并在研究车辆karl上进行了真实世界部署并报告了结果。

Conclusion: 该混合架构能够融合机器学习在复杂场景中的行为生成能力与经典方法在可验证性、确定性和安全保证方面的优势，为安全可信的自动驾驶运动规划提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Recent research in machine and deep learning has shown the potential of learningbased motion planning approaches to improve the driving behavior of automated vehicles, especially in complex environments. However, their complex nature and lack of transparency can hinder explainability and trustworthiness and complicate safety assurance. Motivated by these challenges, we propose a hybrid planning architecture that combines the advantages of machine learning with the verifiability and the determinism of classical approaches. Specifically, we developed a deep neural network to interpret complex traffic scenes and propose driving behavior, while an optimization-based supervision layer validates this proposal and enforces explicit drivability and safety constraints. We evaluate the learned planner's driving behavior in open-loop studies on real-world urban data, discuss system integration aspects for stable closed-loop operation, and report results from real-world deployment on our research vehicle karl..

</details>

### [112] [A multiscale theory based on metabolic scaling connects forest dynamics to tree-size distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11918)
*Christian Grilletta,Tommaso Anfodillo,Gaia Pasqualotto,Samir Suweis,Andrea Rinaldo,Amos Maritan,Davide Bernardi*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个最小化的空间显式动力学框架，用于解释森林群落中物种大小、丰度与资源可用性之间的标度关系。通过解析求解树木大小分布，发现其稳态呈现两个不同指数控制的幂律区间，跨越点由种子注入与局部资源可用性的相互作用决定。此外，边界扰动可打破物种间的生态平衡并产生远超单株植物扩散范围的深层影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 种群大小、丰度与资源可用性之间的标度关系是生态学中最稳健的经验规律之一，但缺乏能够从生态过程机制层面解释这些群落级定律如何涌现的理论框架。

Method: 构建一个最小化的空间显式动力学模型，包含种子扩散、受局部光照限制的生长、局部竞争以及基于代谢标度原理的全局资源约束；通过解析推导获得树木大小分布的解析解。

Result: 稳态树大小分布呈现两个不同的幂律区间，其指数由资源竞争与空间竞争的相对强度决定；跨越点在种子注入与局部资源可用性之间建立联系；边界扰动可诱导效应传播至森林内部深处，远超单株植物的扩散范围。

Conclusion: 该框架为森林标度规律提供了统一的动力学视角，建立了大小分布标度指数与森林状况之间的显式联系，并具有推广到广泛生物群落的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Scaling relations linking species size, abundance, and resource availability are among the most robust empirical regularities in ecology. However, a mechanistic explanation for how these community-level laws emerge from ecological processes remains elusive. Here, we address this gap by developing a minimal spatially explicit dynamical framework for forest communities that incorporates seed dispersal, growth limited by local light availability, local competition, and global resource constraints grounded in metabolic scaling principles. By deriving an analytical solution for the tree-size distribution, we show that its stationary state exhibits two distinct power-law regimes whose exponents are controlled by the relative strength of resource and spatial competition. The crossover between these regimes is set by the interplay between seed injection and local resource availability, establishing an explicit link between the scaling exponent of the size distribution and forest condition. Finally, we show that boundary disturbances can break the ecological balance between competing species and induce effects that propagate deeply into the forest bulk, far beyond the single-plant dispersal range. Together, these results provide a unifying dynamical perspective on forest scaling laws with potential applications to a broad range of biological communities.

</details>


### [113] [Dynamics of fluctuating populations in multi-state switching environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12208)
*Mauro Mobilia*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该论文研究在多状态随机切换环境中两种菌株（一株生长速度略慢）在同一资源上的竞争动力学。这类多状态模型被解释为"盛宴-饥荒"循环的粗粒度描述，通过计算和解析方法刻画种群规模统计、固定概率和平均固定时间，并与二元环境对比，阐明环境波动频率和幅度如何影响种群动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微生物种群通常在波动的环境中进化，传统上常用二元切换模型（资源在丰富与匮乏间突然切换）来描述。然而实验研究表明，真实的"盛宴-饥荒"环境具有更复杂的时变动力学特征，包含多个中间态而非简单的二元切换。因此需要更精细的多状态模型来准确刻画资源逐渐恢复和耗竭过程中的菌株竞争。

Method: 构建了包含有限个中间状态的多状态随机切换模型，每个状态具有各自的承载力（carrying capacity），状态间的切换模拟营养物质可用性的逐渐变化。通过计算方法和解析方法（包括种群规模统计量、固定概率、平均固定时间等分析）对两菌株竞争动力学进行表征，并系统研究切换速率和承载力分布对种群动态的影响。

Result: 论文表征了多状态波动环境中的种群动态，揭示了切换速率和承载力分布如何影响种群规模统计、固定概率和平均固定时间。通过与二元环境结果的对比，阐明了环境波动频率（切换速率）和幅度（承载力分布）对粗粒度"盛宴-饥荒"循环中种群动力学的影响方式，展示了多状态模型与二元模型之间的差异。

Conclusion: 多状态随机切换模型提供了比二元切换模型更符合实验观察的"盛宴-饥荒"环境粗粒度描述，能够更准确地刻画资源逐渐变化下的菌株竞争动态。研究明确了环境波动频率和幅度对种群动态的关键作用，为理解微生物在复杂波动环境中的进化提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Microbial populations generally evolve in fluctuating environments under time-varying conditions. These are often described by binary switching models, sometimes seen as coarse-grained feast-famine cycles, in which resource availability switches abruptly between abundant and scarce conditions. However, experimental studies suggest that feast-famine environments actually exhibit more complex temporal dynamics. Here, we study how two strains, one growing slightly slower than the other, compete for the same resources in fluctuating environments comprising a finite number of intermediate states, each having its own carrying capacity. Environmental switching between these states and their carrying capacities represents gradual changes in nutrient availability. This class of multi-state stochastic switching models can be interpreted as a coarse-grained description of feast--famine cycles and allows us to investigate strain competition under the gradual recovery and depletion of resources. By computational and analytical means, we characterise the population dynamics in these multi-state fluctuating environments. In particular, we study how the switching rates and distribution of carrying capacities affect the population-size statistics, fixation probability, and mean fixation time. By comparing these results with their counterparts in binary environments, we clarify how the frequency and amplitude of environmental fluctuations influence population dynamics in coarse-grained feast-famine cycles.

</details>

### [114] [A determination of the backscattering probability of low-energy antiprotons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11795)
*GBAR Collaboration,K. Park,E. Perez,P. Adrich,I. Belosevic,P. Cladé,M. Chung,P. Comini,P. Crivelli,P. Debu,A. Douillet,S. Geffroy,S. Guellati-Khelifa,P. Guichard,P. -A. Hervieux,L. Hilico,P. Indelicato,S. Jonsell,J. -P. Karr,B. Kim,S. Kim,E. -S. Kim,N. Kuroda,B. Lee,L. Liszkay,D. Lunney,G. Manfredi,B. Mansoulié,V. Martimort,M. Matusiak,V. Nesvizhevsky,F. Nez,N. Paul,P. Pérez,C. Regenfus,C. Roumegou,J. -Y. Roussé,F. Schmidt-Kaler,K. Szymczyk,T. A. Tanaka,B. Tuchming,D. -P. van der Werf,D. Won,S. Wronka,P. Yzombard*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了低能（4和6 keV）反质子撞击材料表面时的背散射概率。传统上假设反质子会在撞击材料表面时立即与原子核湮灭，但本文指出在几个keV的动能下，反质子可能像低能质子、电子或正电子一样发生大角度库仑散射而"背散射"出材料表面。作者通过实验测定了GBAR实验中用于束流成像的微通道板探测器表面的反质子背散射概率，未发现背散射证据，在68%置信水平下设定上限为14%。该结果对确定GBAR实验中参与反氢原子产生的反质子数目具有重要意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GBAR（反重力）实验中，精确测定反氢原子产生截面依赖于对入射反质子数目的精确计数。如果反质子在探测器表面发生背散射，而这一现象被忽略，将导致对参与反氢产生过程的反质子数目估计偏差，从而影响测量精度。由于此前对低能反质子背散射概率几乎未知，需要专门的实验测定。

Method: 在GBAR实验中，将4 keV和6 keV的反质子束流射向用于束流成像的微通道板（MCP）探测器表面，通过直接实验测量反质子的背散射概率。分析探测信号以寻找背散射事件，并采用统计方法在未观察到显著背散射信号的情况下给出上限。

Result: 实验未发现反质子背散射的证据。在68%置信水平下，反质子背散射概率的上限被设定为14%。同时，论文讨论了该结果对确定GBAR实验中参与反氢产生的反质子数目的影响。

Conclusion: 低能（keV量级）反质子在MCP探测器表面的背散射概率被限制在14%（68%置信水平）以内。该上限结果表明，在GBAR实验的当前精度需求下，背散射效应可能不会造成显著的计数系统误差，但仍需在相关分析中加以考虑。

Abstract: It is commonly assumed that antiprotons impinging on a material surface annihilate promptly with the nuclei of the material. However, at kinetic energies of a few keV, this assumption may not hold. As with low-energy protons, electrons or positrons that can be reflected from a target, they may undergo large-angle Coulomb scattering before annihilation occurs, thereby appearing to be "backscattered" from the material surface. This backscattering fraction, largely unknown, is a crucial ingredient to the determination of the production cross-section of antihydrogen atoms in the GBAR experiment. This paper presents a determination of the probability that 4 and 6 keV antiprotons backscatter on the surface of a Micro-Channel Plate detector used for beam imaging at GBAR. No evidence for backscattering has been found and an upper limit of 14% at 68% confidence level has been set on this probability. The impact of backscattering on the determination of the number of antiprotons that participate in antihydrogen production in GBAR is also addressed.

</details>


### [115] [Toward a Measurement of the Higgs Boson Mass with Natural-Width Precision at FCC-ee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12071)
*Jan Eysermans,Ang Li,Gregorio Bernardi,Christoph Paus,Emmanuel Perez,Michele Selvaggi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了FCC-ee通过leptonic ZH反冲通道可将Higgs玻色子质量测量精度提升至4 MeV（包含统计与系统不确定度），从而实现了电子Yukawa耦合的共振探测，并明确了所需的探测器与加速器性能要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higgs玻色子亚10 MeV精度的质量测量可使Higgs玻色子耦合的测定达到亚百分比精度，并防止Higgs质量成为电弱拟合的瓶颈输入；同时，探测电子Yukawa耦合需要与Higgs自然宽度（约4 MeV）相当的质量测量精度，因此推动更高精度的质量测量至关重要。

Method: 利用leptonic ZH反冲通道（Z玻色子衰变为轻子对），通过反冲质量方法重建Higgs玻色子质量，并综合统计与系统不确定度评估FCC-ee的实验潜力，同时分析达到该精度所需的探测器（如径迹系统、电磁量能器）和加速器（如束斑尺寸、能量标定）性能。

Result: FCC-ee在leptonic ZH反冲通道中可实现Higgs玻色子质量测量精度达4 MeV，该精度与Higgs自然宽度相当，足以支持电子Yukawa耦合的共振探测；研究进一步给出了达成此精度所必需的探测器与加速器性能指标。

Conclusion: FCC-ee具有将Higgs玻色子质量测量精度推进到4 MeV的可行性，这将使Higgs质量不再限制电弱拟合，并首次实现通过共振产生探测电子Yukawa耦合，为精确理解Higgs物理提供关键实验途径。

Abstract: Higgs boson mass measurements with sub-10 MeV precision enable sub-percent determinations of Higgs boson couplings and prevent the Higgs boson mass from becoming a limiting input to electroweak fits. Probing the electron Yukawa coupling through resonant Higgs boson production requires a precision comparable to the Higgs boson natural width of approximately 4 MeV. Using the leptonic ZH recoil channels, we show that FCC-ee can reach a Higgs boson mass precision of 4 MeV, including statistical and systematic uncertainties, thereby enabling this unique measurement. We identify the detector and accelerator performance required to reach this precision.

</details>


### [116] [Laboratory constraints on peV-scale mass splitting between ordinary and sterile neutron states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12173)
*N. J. Ayres,Z. Berezhiani,G. Bison,K. Bodek,V. Bondar,P. -J. Chiu,M. Daum,C. B. Doorenbos,S. Emmenegger,K. Kirch,V. Kletzl,J. Krempel,B. Lauss,D. Pais,I. Rienäcker,D. Ries,D. Rozpędzik,P. Schmidt-Wellenburg,K. S. Tanaka,J. Zejma,N. Ziehl,G. Zsigmond*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文研究中子与镜像中子（n-n'）振荡，在非质量简并的情况下，通过调节磁场（5–360 μT）匹配有限质量劈裂的共振条件，推导出n-n'振荡时间常数的排除限，达到约20秒，覆盖质量差0.3–22 peV范围，部分参数区间优于中子星冷却的天体物理约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镜像扇区作为平行"镜子"世界的物质状态可能构成宇宙中的暗物质。此前对n-n'振荡的研究多假设两个状态质量简并，而现实中可能存在有限质量劈裂，因此需要探索非简并情形下的参数空间，以提供更全面的实验约束。

Method: 作者考虑镜像扇区内的相互作用，在n和n'非质量简并的假设下，将外加磁场调节至5–360 μT范围以匹配有限质量劈裂的共振条件，系统地扫描参数空间并推导振荡时间常数的排除限。

Result: 在质量差0.3–22 peV范围内，n-n'振荡时间常数的排除限达到约20秒。在部分参数范围内，该限制超过模型依赖的中子星冷却约束，首次在此场景下提供了比天体物理估计更严格的实验约束。

Conclusion: 该工作通过实验手段在非质量简并情形下给出了比中子星冷却界更紧的n-n'振荡约束，为镜像暗物质模型提供了重要的实验限制，是该参数空间内第一个超越天体物理估计的实验验证。

Abstract: Sterile states of matter, represented by a parallel ``mirror'' sector, may contribute to the observed dark matter in the Universe. We investigated the parameter space of neutron $(n)$ to mirror-neutron $(n')$ oscillations, in the case where the two states are not necessarily mass-degenerate, taking into account interactions in the mirror sector. By tuning the magnitude of an applied magnetic-field in the range $5~μ\mathrm{T} < B < 360~μ\mathrm{T}$ to corresponding resonance conditions for finite mass splitting, we derive exclusion limits for the $n-n'$ oscillation time constant reaching about $20~\text{s}$ over the mass-difference range $0.3 - 22~\text{peV}$. In parts of this parameter range, our limits exceed the model-dependent neutron-star-cooling bound, providing the first experimental constraints in this scenario that are more stringent than this astrophysical estimate.

</details>

Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,T. Zies,D. Zuliani,X. Zuo*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb实验在7、8和13 TeV下采集的8.4 fb⁻¹质子-质子碰撞数据，对B⁰→K*⁰(→K⁺π⁻)μ⁺μ⁻衰变振幅进行了分析，通过Legendre多项式在1.1<q²<8.0 GeV²/c⁴区间构造振幅，提取CP平均观测量。部分观测量相对于标准模型存在偏差，可解释为有效Wilson系数的偏移。该模型无关方法允许灵活的q²分箱选择，取决于分箱方案，Wilson系数C₉偏离标准模型预期的显著性在4.3σ到4.8σ之间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型对稀有B介子衰变有精确预言，但实验观测到的某些角观测量与之存在偏差，可能暗示超出标准模型的新物理。作者希望通过模型无关的振幅分析来区分强子效应与新物理贡献。

Method: 利用LHCb在7、8、13 TeV能级下采集的8.4 fb⁻¹数据，选取B⁰→K*⁰(→K⁺π⁻)μ⁺μ⁻衰变通道，在1.1<q²<8.0 GeV²/c⁴区间用Legendre多项式构造衰变振幅，从中提取CP平均观测量，并以模型无关方式将偏差解释为有效Wilson系数的偏移。

Result: 部分CP平均观测量偏离标准模型预言。将偏差解释为Wilson系数的偏移后，C₉偏离标准模型预期的显著性在4.3σ至4.8σ之间，具体取决于所选择的q²分箱方案。

Conclusion: 该模型无关的振幅分析方法为测试理论预言提供了灵活性，有助于区分强子效应与潜在的新物理贡献。观察到的C₉偏差（4.3σ-4.8σ）为超出标准模型的现象提供了有意义的线索，但尚未达到决定性发现的显著性水平。

Abstract: An analysis of the decay amplitudes of $B^0 \to K^{*0}(\to K^+π^-)μ^+μ^-$ is presented, using proton-proton collision data recorded by the LHCb experiment at centre-of-mass energies of 7, 8, and 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 8.4 fb$^{-1}$. The amplitudes are constructed from Legendre polynomials in the $μ^+μ^-$ invariant mass squared region $1.1<q^2<8.0$ GeV$^2/c^4$. $C\!P$-averaged observables are obtained from the amplitudes. Some of these observables present deviations with respect to the Standard Model, which can be interpreted as shifts in the effective Wilson coefficients. This model-independent approach enables tests of theoretical predictions that can help disentangle hadronic effects from potential contributions from physics beyond the Standard Model. This allows flexibility in the choice of $q^2$ binning for global analyses. Depending on the binning scheme, the deviation of the Wilson coefficient $C_9$ from its Standard Model expectation varies from $4.3σ$ to $4.8σ$.

</details>


### [118] [Measurement of the $\bar ν_μ-$Hydrogen Charged-Current Quasi-Elastic Cross Section using the NOvA Near Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12293)
*The NOvA Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用NOvA近探测器在NuMI束流中1.2×10^21质子打靶的数据，测量了μ子反中微子与氢的带电电流准弹性散射总截面，得到σ(ν̄_μH→μ^+n)=0.538±0.009(统计)±0.010(系统)±0.055(通量)×10^-38 cm²，平均能量为1.9 GeV，这是该过程迄今最精确的总截面测量。测量结果统计与非通量系统不确定度比通量不确定度小四倍以上，可用于约束绝对反中微子通量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子-氢（单核子）相互作用是理解中微子-核子散射动力学的基础过程，对中微子振荡测量和核模型验证具有重要意义。目前缺乏高统计量的反中微子-氢截面数据，需要精确测量以改善对反中微子通量的约束和理论模型检验。

Method: 利用NOvA近探测器在NuMI束流中获得1.2×10^21质子打靶曝光数据，通过基于拓扑和运动学约束的事例挑选获得35,509个信号事例（氢气含量10.8%的探测器）。使用专门的data control samples约束较重核上的（反）中微子相互作用的背景，显著降低相关系统不确定度。最终得到平均能量1.9 GeV下的总截面测量值。

Result: 测得σ(ν̄_μH→μ^+n)=0.538±0.009(stat)±0.010(syst)±0.055(flux)×10^-38 cm²，平均能量1.9 GeV。这是迄今最精确的反微子-氢准弹性散射总截面测量，统计量（35,509个信号事例）为目前最高。组合统计与非通量系统不确定度比通量不确定度小四倍以上。

Conclusion: 该测量提供了前所未有的精确反中微子-氢散射总截面数据，其结果可为未来约束绝对反中微子通量提供重要依据，并有助于检验中微子-核子相互作用理论模型。

Abstract: We report a measurement of the total cross section for muon antineutrino charged-current quasi-elastic scattering on hydrogen, $\bar ν_μ{\rm H} \to μ^+ n$, in the NOvA near detector using a $1.2\times10^{21}$ proton-on-target exposure in the NuMI beam. A selection based on topological and kinematic constraints yields 35,509 signal events in the hydrogen-rich ($10.8\%$) detector, providing the highest statistics of (anti)neutrino--hydrogen interactions measured to date. Backgrounds from (anti)neutrino interactions on heavier nuclei are constrained using dedicated data control samples, significantly reducing the related systematic uncertainties. We obtain a value $σ(\bar ν_μ{\rm H} \to μ^+ n) = 0.538 \pm 0.009 ({\rm stat}) \pm 0.010 ({\rm syst}) \pm0.055 ({\rm flux}) \times 10^{-38}$ cm$^2$ for the total cross section at an average energy of 1.9 GeV, the most precise total cross-section measurement of this process to date. The combined statistical and non-flux systematic uncertainty is more than four times smaller than the flux uncertainty, allowing a future use of this measurement to constrain the absolute $\bar ν_μ$ flux.

</details>
